# Skills · C · part 26

881 entries, cdrom-internal-decoder to cekura-eval-design. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cdrom-internal-decoder**: PSX CDROM internal: CXD1815Q sub-CPU configuration/status/address/misc registers, servo/signal processor commands (CXA1782BR, CXD2510Q, CXD2545Q, CXD2938Q), coefficient tables. Use when working with CDROM decoder/servo internals or chipset-specific commands. ([sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin](https://github.com/sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/cdrom-internal-decoder) · ★2)
- **cdrom-internal-hc05**: PSX CDROM internal: Motorola MC68HC05 8-bit sub-CPU instruction set, on-chip I/O ports (port A-D, timer, SCI, SPI), PSX-specific I/O usage, selftest mode for 52pin/80pin chips. Use when reverse-engineering CDROM controller firmware or sub-CPU behavior. ([sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin](https://github.com/sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/cdrom-internal-hc05) · ★2)
- **cdrom-io-commands**: PSX CDROM drive: I/O port registers (index 0-3), command protocol, control/seek/read/status/audio commands with parameters and responses. Use when working with CDROM I/O, reading sectors, seeking, or playing CD audio. ([sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin](https://github.com/sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/cdrom-io-commands) · ★2)
- **cdrom-test-protection**: PSX CDROM drive: test commands (version/region/chipset/SCEx), secret unlock, Video CD commands, response timing and queueing, mainloop behavior. ([sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin](https://github.com/sadnescity/psx-spx-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/cdrom-test-protection) · ★2)

- **cdrom-xa-iso**: CDROM XA and ISO: XA subheader and ADPCM audio compression, ISO9660 volume/file/directory descriptors, Joliet extension, PSX copy protection (SCEx strings, modchips, LibCrypt). - **cds**: Coherence-Driven Software. Use for engineering work — code, tests, branches, diffs, CI, releases, deployments — that requires structurally-enforced discipline against artifact degradation under unmeasured churn. Software-development realization of CCNF; sibling to cnos.cdr (research). ([usurobor/cnos](https://github.com/usurobor/cnos/tree/HEAD/src/packages/cnos.cds/skills/cds) · ★9 · 2 copies)
